<span class="ct-node-title-full">Roswell: Mystery, Evidence, and the 1947 Crash</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">The Roswell UFO crash is one of the most famous alleged extraterrestrial incidents in modern history. In July 1947, debris found on a ranch near Roswell, New Mexico, led the U.S. Army Air Forces to briefly announce that it had recovered a “flying disc” before quickly changing its explanation to a weather balloon.</span>
